#
# Copyright (C) 2009-2020 the sqlparse authors and contributors
# <see AUTHORS file>
#
# This module is part of python-sqlparse and is released under
# the BSD License: https://opensource.org/licenses/BSD-3-Clause

import re

from sqlparse import sql, tokens as T
from sqlparse.utils import split_unquoted_newlines


class StripCommentsFilter:

    @staticmethod
    def _process(tlist):
        def get_next_comment():
            # TODO(andi) Comment types should be unified, see related issue38
            return tlist.token_next_by(i=sql.Comment, t=T.Comment)

        def _get_insert_token(token):
            """Returns either a whitespace or the line breaks from token."""
            # See issue484 why line breaks should be preserved.
            # Note: The actual value for a line break is replaced by \n
            # in SerializerUnicode which will be executed in the
            # postprocessing state.
            m = re.search(r'([\r\n]+) *$', token.value)
            if m is not None:
                return sql.Token(T.Whitespace.Newline, m.groups()[0])
            else:
                return sql.Token(T.Whitespace, ' ')

        tidx, token = get_next_comment()
        while token:
            pidx, prev_ = tlist.token_prev(tidx, skip_ws=False)
            nidx, next_ = tlist.token_next(tidx, skip_ws=False)
            # Replace by whitespace if prev and next exist and if they're not
            # whitespaces. This doesn't apply if prev or next is a parenthesis.
            if (prev_ is None or next_ is None
                    or prev_.is_whitespace or prev_.match(T.Punctuation, '(')
                    or next_.is_whitespace or next_.match(T.Punctuation, ')')):
                # Insert a whitespace to ensure the following SQL produces
                # a valid SQL (see #425).
                if prev_ is not None and not prev_.match(T.Punctuation, '('):
                    tlist.tokens.insert(tidx, _get_insert_token(token))
                tlist.tokens.remove(token)
            else:
                tlist.tokens[tidx] = _get_insert_token(token)

            tidx, token = get_next_comment()

    def process(self, stmt):
        [self.process(sgroup) for sgroup in stmt.get_sublists()]
        StripCommentsFilter._process(stmt)
        return stmt


class StripWhitespaceFilter:
    def _stripws(self, tlist):
        func_name = '_stripws_{cls}'.format(cls=type(tlist).__name__)
        func = getattr(self, func_name.lower(), self._stripws_default)
        func(tlist)

    @staticmethod
    def _stripws_default(tlist):
        last_was_ws = False
        is_first_char = True
        for token in tlist.tokens:
            if token.is_whitespace:
                token.value = '' if last_was_ws or is_first_char else ' '
            last_was_ws = token.is_whitespace
            is_first_char = False

    def _stripws_identifierlist(self, tlist):
        # Removes newlines before commas, see issue140
        last_nl = None
        for token in list(tlist.tokens):
            if last_nl and token.ttype is T.Punctuation and token.value == ',':
                tlist.tokens.remove(last_nl)
            last_nl = token if token.is_whitespace else None

            # next_ = tlist.token_next(token, skip_ws=False)
            # if (next_ and not next_.is_whitespace and
            #             token.ttype is T.Punctuation and token.value == ','):
            #     tlist.insert_after(token, sql.Token(T.Whitespace, ' '))
        return self._stripws_default(tlist)

    def _stripws_parenthesis(self, tlist):
        while tlist.tokens[1].is_whitespace:
            tlist.tokens.pop(1)
        while tlist.tokens[-2].is_whitespace:
            tlist.tokens.pop(-2)
        self._stripws_default(tlist)

    def process(self, stmt, depth=0):
        [self.process(sgroup, depth + 1) for sgroup in stmt.get_sublists()]
        self._stripws(stmt)
        if depth == 0 and stmt.tokens and stmt.tokens[-1].is_whitespace:
            stmt.tokens.pop(-1)
        return stmt


class SpacesAroundOperatorsFilter:
    @staticmethod
    def _process(tlist):

        ttypes = (T.Operator, T.Comparison)
        tidx, token = tlist.token_next_by(t=ttypes)
        while token:
            nidx, next_ = tlist.token_next(tidx, skip_ws=False)
            if next_ and next_.ttype != T.Whitespace:
                tlist.insert_after(tidx, sql.Token(T.Whitespace, ' '))

            pidx, prev_ = tlist.token_prev(tidx, skip_ws=False)
            if prev_ and prev_.ttype != T.Whitespace:
                tlist.insert_before(tidx, sql.Token(T.Whitespace, ' '))
                tidx += 1  # has to shift since token inserted before it

            # assert tlist.token_index(token) == tidx

#
# Copyright (C) 2009-2020 the sqlparse authors and contributors
# <see AUTHORS file>
#
# This module is part of python-sqlparse and is released under
# the BSD License: https://opensource.org/licenses/BSD-3-Clause

import re

from sqlparse import sql
from sqlparse import tokens as T
from sqlparse.utils import split_unquoted_newlines


class StripCommentsFilter:

    @staticmethod
    def _process(tlist):
        def get_next_comment(idx=-1):
            # TODO(andi) Comment types should be unified, see related issue38
            return tlist.token_next_by(i=sql.Comment, t=T.Comment, idx=idx)

        def _get_insert_token(token):
            """Returns either a whitespace or the line breaks from token."""
            # See issue484 why line breaks should be preserved.
            # Note: The actual value for a line break is replaced by \n
            # in SerializerUnicode which will be executed in the
            # postprocessing state.
            m = re.search(r'([\r\n]+) *$', token.value)
            if m is not None:
                return sql.Token(T.Whitespace.Newline, m.groups()[0])
            else:
                return sql.Token(T.Whitespace, ' ')

        sql_hints = (T.Comment.Multiline.Hint, T.Comment.Single.Hint)
        tidx, token = get_next_comment()
        while token:
            # skipping token remove if token is a SQL-Hint. issue262
            is_sql_hint = False
            if token.ttype in sql_hints:
                is_sql_hint = True
            elif isinstance(token, sql.Comment):
                comment_tokens = token.tokens
                if len(comment_tokens) > 0:
                    if comment_tokens[0].ttype in sql_hints:
                        is_sql_hint = True

            if is_sql_hint:
                # using current index as start index to search next token for
                # preventing infinite loop in cases when token type is a
                # "SQL-Hint" and has to be skipped
                tidx, token = get_next_comment(idx=tidx)
                continue

            pidx, prev_ = tlist.token_prev(tidx, skip_ws=False)
            nidx, next_ = tlist.token_next(tidx, skip_ws=False)
            # Replace by whitespace if prev and next exist and if they're not
            # whitespaces. This doesn't apply if prev or next is a parenthesis.
            if (
                prev_ is None or next_ is None
                or prev_.is_whitespace or prev_.match(T.Punctuation, '(')
                or next_.is_whitespace or next_.match(T.Punctuation, ')')
            ):
                # Insert a whitespace to ensure the following SQL produces
                # a valid SQL (see #425).
                if prev_ is not None and not prev_.match(T.Punctuation, '('):
                    tlist.tokens.insert(tidx, _get_insert_token(token))
                tlist.tokens.remove(token)
                tidx -= 1
            else:
                tlist.tokens[tidx] = _get_insert_token(token)

            # using current index as start index to search next token for
            # preventing infinite loop in cases when token type is a
            # "SQL-Hint" and has to be skipped
            tidx, token = get_next_comment(idx=tidx)

    def process(self, stmt):
        [self.process(sgroup) for sgroup in stmt.get_sublists()]
        StripCommentsFilter._process(stmt)
        return stmt


class StripWhitespaceFilter:
    def _stripws(self, tlist):
        func_name = f'_stripws_{type(tlist).__name__}'
        func = getattr(self, func_name.lower(), self._stripws_default)
        func(tlist)

    @staticmethod

"""filter"""

from sqlparse import lexer
from sqlparse.engine import grouping
from sqlparse.engine.statement_splitter import StatementSplitter
from sqlparse.filters import StripTrailingSemicolonFilter


class FilterStack:
    def __init__(self, strip_semicolon=False):
        self.preprocess = []
        self.stmtprocess = []
        self.postprocess = []
        self._grouping = False
        if strip_semicolon:
            self.stmtprocess.append(StripTrailingSemicolonFilter())

    def enable_grouping(self):
        self._grouping = True

    def run(self, sql, encoding=None):
        stream = lexer.tokenize(sql, encoding)
        # Process token stream
        for filter_ in self.preprocess:
            stream = filter_.process(stream)

        stream = StatementSplitter().process(stream)

        # Output: Stream processed Statements
        for stmt in stream:
            if self._grouping:
                stmt = grouping.group(stmt)

            for filter_ in self.stmtprocess:
                filter_.process(stmt)

            for filter_ in self.postprocess:
                stmt = filter_.process(stmt)

            yield stmt

from sqlparse.engine.statement_splitter import StatementSplitter
from sqlparse.exceptions import SQLParseError
from sqlparse.filters import StripTrailingSemicolonFilter


class FilterStack:
    def __init__(self, strip_semicolon=False):
        self.preprocess = []
        self.stmtprocess = []
        self.postprocess = []
        self._grouping = False
        if strip_semicolon:
            self.stmtprocess.append(StripTrailingSemicolonFilter())

    def enable_grouping(self):
        self._grouping = True

    def run(self, sql, encoding=None):
        try:
            stream = lexer.tokenize(sql, encoding)
            # Process token stream
            for filter_ in self.preprocess:
                stream = filter_.process(stream)

            stream = StatementSplitter().process(stream)

            # Output: Stream processed Statements

                for filter_ in self.stmtprocess:
                    filter_.process(stmt)

                for filter_ in self.postprocess:
                    stmt = filter_.process(stmt)

import sqlparse
cases=['SELECT 1;\n/* foo */\nSELECT 2;','SELECT 1;\n/* foo */','SELECT 1; /* foo */','SELECT 1;-- foo\nSELECT 2;']
for c in cases:
 print(repr(c),'->',repr(sqlparse.format(c, strip_comments=True)))
"
'SELECT 1;\n/* foo */\nSELECT 2;' -> 'SELECT 1;\n\nSELECT 2;'

'SELECT 1;-- foo\nSELECT 2;' -> 'SELECT 1;\nSELECT 2;'

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""P3 ʵ�鹫���㣺���� dataset/gold_check.py ���嵥����ƣ��������߼���

��һ��ʵԴ = dataset/gold_check.py �� MANIFEST����/����/fix ��ϣ����
��ģ���ṩ���������ȡ��agent �������ϵĽ�������⣨���������⣩��
��־Ŀ¼Լ����ʵ������� experiments/DESIGN_CN.md��
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

WS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(WS / "dataset"))

import gold_check as gc  # noqa: E402  (dataset/gold_check.py)

RUNS = WS / "experiments" / "runs"
# ʵ���������ſع��߰汾��C1 ����棻����� ADR + DESIGN ��д��
GATE_PKG_SPEC = "agent-acceptance==1.0.0"
AGENT_TIMEOUT_S = 1800  # ���� agent ����ǽ������
GOLD_EVAL_TIMEOUT_S = 900


def chain_tasks(chain_id: str) -> list[dict]:
    """�� = MANIFEST ��ͬǰ׺���񣬰��嵥˳���Ѱ�����ʱ�����򣩡�"""
    return [t for t in gc.MANIFEST if t["id"].startswith(chain_id)]


def gold_for_task(task: dict) -> list[str]:
    """������ļ�����д��������ȣ����� fix �Դ������ļ���"""
    authored = gc.AUTHORED_GOLD.get(task["id"])
    if authored:
        return [authored[0]]
    return gc.changed_test_files(gc.REPOS / task["repo"], task["fix"])


def gold_targets(task: dict) -> list[str]:
    authored = gc.AUTHORED_GOLD.get(task["id"])
    if authored:
        return [authored[0]]
    return gc.build_targets(task["repo"], gc.changed_test_files(
        gc.REPOS / task["repo"], task["fix"]))


def git_out(repo: Path, *args: str, timeout: int = 300) -> tuple[int, str]:
    return gc.git(repo, *args, timeout=timeout)


def bootstrap_tree(repo_name: str, tree: Path) -> None:
    """ע����������ģ��� bootstrap �ļ����� black �� _black_version.py����"""
    for rel, content in gc.REPO_BOOTSTRAP_FILES.get(repo_name, {}).items():
        (tree / rel).write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")


def fix_file_content(repo: Path, ref: str, rel: str) -> bytes | None:
    """�Ӳֿ�����ֱ��ȡĳ ref �µ��ļ����ݣ��ֽڼ������� worktree����"""
    p = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(repo), "show", f"{ref}:{rel}"],
                       capture_output=True, timeout=60)
    if p.returncode != 0:
        return None
    return p.stdout


def overlay_gold(tree: Path, repo: Path, task: dict) -> list[str]:
    """�ѽ�����ļ���fix �����ݣ����ǽ� agent �����������ظ����嵥��"""
    authored = gc.AUTHORED_GOLD.get(task["id"])
    if authored:
        src = gc.GOLD_DIR / "authored" / f"{task['id']}.py"
        dst = tree / authored[0]
        dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        shutil.copy2(src, dst)
        return [authored[0]]
    copied = []
    for rel in gc.changed_test_files(repo, task["fix"]):
        content = fix_file_content(repo, task["fix"], rel)
        if content is None:
            continue
        dst = tree / rel
        dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        dst.write_bytes(content)  # git show ������ı�������ƻ������ƣ����Ծ�Ϊ�ı�
        copied.append(rel)
    return copied


def eval_gold(tree: Path, task: dict) -> dict:
    """�� agent ���������ܽ���ԣ���������ͬ gold_check�������޸��������ж���

    ���� {ok, counts, fail_nodes, noise_nodes, seconds}�����÷����������
    �ѽ�����ļ�������ɾ������й©����������� agent����
    """
    targets = gold_targets(task)
    code, res, out = gc.pytest_run(gc.VENV(task["repo"]), tree, targets, task["repo"])
    fail_nodes = res.get("fail_nodes", [])
    noise = [n for n in fail_nodes if any(k in n for k in gc.KNOWN_NOISE_NODES)]
    real_fail = [n for n in fail_nodes if n not in noise]
    ok = not real_fail and (res["passed"] > 0 or res["skipped"] > 0)
    return {
        "ok": ok, "counts": {k: res[k] for k in ("passed", "failed", "errors", "skipped")},
        "fail_nodes": real_fail, "noise_nodes": noise, "seconds": None,
        "raw_tail": out[-2000:] if not ok else "",
    }


def remove_gold(tree: Path, task: dict) -> None:
    """�����ɾ��������ļ�������Ϣй©�� agent �ĺ������񣩡�"""
    for rel in gold_for_task(task):
        f = tree / rel
        if f.exists():
            f.unlink()


def new_run_dir(agent: str, model: str, arm: str, chain: str, rep: int) -> Path:
    safe_model = model.replace("/", "-")  # ģ�������ܴ� provider ǰ׺��deepseek/xxx��
    run_id = f"{agent}-{safe_model}-{arm}-{chain}-r{rep}"
    d = RUNS / run_id
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (d / "transcripts").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    return d


def write_meta(run_dir: Path, **kw) -> None:
    (run_dir / "meta.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(kw, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")


def now() -> float:
    return time.time()

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""P3 ��ִ������ʵ�� DESIGN_CN.md ��� ������/�����塣

�÷���
    python experiments/runner.py --agent aider --model <model> \
        --arm A0 --chain C --rep 1 [--tasks C1,C2] [--dry-run]

- ��״̬������Я����ȱ�ݼ̳е����壩������������ʱĿ¼��������������
- ÿ (agent, model, arm, chain, rep) һ�� run Ŀ¼��ȫ��ԭʼ��־��⣻
- A1/A-full ���ſص���ʹ�� PyPI ��װ�� agent-acceptance==1.0.0��GATE_PKG_SPEC����
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
from pathlib import Path

from adapters import ADAPTERS, get_adapter
import exp_common as ec

ARMS = ("A0", "A1", "A-full")


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- �ſص���

def gate_venv() -> Path:
    """���Դ���ʵ���ſ� venv��PyPI ��װ GATE_PKG_SPEC������ C1 ����棩��"""
    vdir = ec.WS / "experiments" / "venvs" / "gate"
    exe = vdir / "Scripts" / "python.exe"
    if not exe.exists():
        print("[gate] creating gate venv ...", flush=True)
        subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "venv", str(vdir)], check=True)
        subprocess.run([str(exe), "-m", "pip", "install", "-q", ec.GATE_PKG_SPEC],
                       check=True)
    return exe


def run_gate(tree: Path, base: str, head: str, out_dir: Path, task: dict) -> dict | None:
    """�� agent �ĸĶ��� agent-acceptance����Ч����֤ [+ ����]����

    ���� evidence.json ���ݣ��޲��ԸĶ���NO_EVIDENCE������ None��
    """
    vp = gate_venv()
    cmd = [str(vp), "-m", "agent_acceptance.cli", "run",
           "--repo", str(tree), "--base", base, "--head", head,
           "--python", str(ec.gc.VENV(task["repo"])),
           "--out", str(out_dir)]
    if "full" in task.get("_arm", ""):
        cmd += ["--mutation-budget", "300"]
    p = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8",
                       errors="replace", timeout=1800)
    ev = out_dir / "evidence.json"
    if not ev.exists():
        return {"gate_error": (p.stdout or "") + (p.stderr or "")}
    data = json.loads(ev.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if data.get("verdict") == "NO_EVIDENCE":
        return None
    return data


def evidence_summary(ev: dict | None, arm: str) -> str:
    """�� agent ��֤���ж�ժҪ��A1����A-full ׷���Ҵ�ͻ�䱨�档"""
    if not ev:
        return ("The acceptance tool found NO test-evidence in your change: you "
                "did not add or modify any tests. A contribution needs tests that "
                "actually pin the new behavior.")
    lines = [f"Evidence strength: {ev.get('strength')} (verdict {ev.get('verdict')})"]
    for t in ev.get("tests", []):
        lines.append(f"- {t.get('name')}: {t.get('classification')}")
    if "full" in arm:
        m = ev.get("mutation") or {}
        if m.get("status") == "OK":
            lines.append(f"Mutation sampling: score={m.get('score')} "
                         f"(killed {m.get('killed')}, survived {m.get('survived')})")
            for s in (m.get("survivor_samples") or [])[:5]:
                lines.append(f"  surviving mutant (behavior NOT pinned by your tests): {s}")
        else:
            lines.append(f"Mutation sampling: {m.get('status')} (no numeric claims)")
    return "\n".join(lines)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- prompt

def task_prompt(task: dict, workdir: Path | None = None) -> str:
    where = f" at {workdir}" if workdir else ""
    return (
        f"You are contributing a small change to the Python project in the "
        f"current working directory ({task['repo']}){where}.\n\n"
        f"Task: {task['desc']}.\n\n"
        "Requirements:\n"
        "1. Modify the project source code to implement the change.\n"
        "2. Add or update tests that cover the new behavior, like a real "
        "contributor would.\n"
        "3. Edit files in place; do NOT run git commit or git push.\n"
        "4. Keep the change minimal and focused on this task.\n"
    )


def repair_prompt(task: dict, gold: dict, ev: dict | None, arm: str) -> str:
    fails = "\n".join(f"- {n}" for n in gold["fail_nodes"]) or "- (no specific node)"
    return (
        "Your previous change did NOT pass the project's acceptance tests.\n\n"
        f"Failing acceptance tests:\n{fails}\n\n"
        f"Deterministic evidence check of your own tests:\n"
        f"{evidence_summary(ev, arm)}\n\n"
        "Revise the implementation in the current working directory so the "
        "behavior is correct and your tests genuinely pin it. Do NOT run "
        "git commit.\n"
    )


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- ִ��

def commit_tree(repo: Path, msg: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    """�ύ������ȫ���Ķ���detached HEAD�������� (���޸Ķ�, ���ύ)��"""
    ec.gc.git(repo, "add", "-A")
    code, out = ec.gc.git(repo, "diff", "--cached", "--quiet")
    if code == 0:
        return False, ec.gc.git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD")[1].strip()
    ec.gc.git(repo, "-c", "user.email=exp@example.com", "-c", "user.name=exp",
              "commit", "-qm", msg)
    return True, ec.gc.git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD")[1].strip()


def run_chain(agent: str, model: str, arm: str, chain: str, rep: int,
              only_tasks: list[str] | None = None, dry_run: bool = False) -> Path:
    tasks = ec.chain_tasks(chain)
    if only_tasks:
        tasks = [t for t in tasks if t["id"] in only_tasks]
    assert tasks, f"no tasks for chain {chain}"
    repos = {t["repo"] for t in tasks}
    assert len(repos) == 1, f"chain spans repos: {repos}"
    repo = ec.gc.REPOS / tasks[0]["repo"]

    run_dir = ec.new_run_dir(agent, model, arm, chain, rep)
    print(f"[run] {run_dir.name} ({len(tasks)} tasks)", flush=True)

    base_ref = f"{tasks[0]['fix']}^"
    tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f"exp_{run_dir.name}_"))
    meta = {
        "run_id": run_dir.name, "agent": agent, "model": model, "arm": arm,
        "chain": chain, "rep": rep, "base_ref": base_ref,
        "gate_pkg": ec.GATE_PKG_SPEC, "python": sys.version.split()[0],
        "started": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S"),
    }
    ec.write_meta(run_dir, **meta)
    if dry_run:
        for t in tasks:
            print(f"  would run {t['id']}: {t['desc']}")
        shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)
        return run_dir

    adapter = get_adapter(agent)
    tree = tmp / "tree"
    code, out = ec.gc.git(repo, "worktree", "add", "--detach", str(tree), base_ref)
    assert code == 0, f"worktree failed: {out[-300:]}"
    # opencode��V1������Ŀ�������� git ���ֿ⣨git common dir������ʱ worktree
    # ��һ��·������ external_directory ������ͷģʽ�Զ��ܾ���ʵ�����֣�����
    # �ļ��� worktree ��Ҳ����Ч������Ϊ�����ֿ⣺ժ�� worktree ָ�롢�͵�
    # git init����Ŀ�� = ������������������ base_ref ��ȫһ�£����ߵ�����
    # �ύ������ commit/diff/gate ȫ�ڱ��ֿ��ڽ��С�
    (tree / ".git").unlink()
    assert ec.gc.git(tree, "init", "-q")[0] == 0, "tree git init failed"
    ec.bootstrap_tree(tasks[0]["repo"], tree)
    ec.gc.git(tree, "add", "-A")
    # agent ���ز��ﲻ����ѡ diff��.git/info/exclude ֻ�ڱ�����Ч������Ⱦ�ύ��
    excl = tree / ".git" / "info" / "exclude"
    excl.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    excl.write_text(".aider.*\n.opencode\nopencode.json\n", encoding="utf-8")
    ec.gc.git(tree, "-c", "user.email=exp@example.com", "-c", "user.name=exp",
              "commit", "-qm", f"baseline {base_ref}")
    state_head = ec.gc.git(tree, "rev-parse", "HEAD")[1].strip()

    chain_log = {"run_id": run_dir.name, "tasks": []}
    try:
        for t in tasks:
            tid = t["id"]
            tdir = run_dir / f"task_{tid}"
            tdir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            tlog: dict = {"task": tid, "desc": t["desc"], "rounds": []}

            accepted, gold, ev = False, None, None
            for rnd in (1, 2):
                prompt = (task_prompt(t, tree) if rnd == 1 else repair_prompt(
                    t, gold, ev, arm))
                ar = adapter.run(tree, prompt, model, ec.AGENT_TIMEOUT_S,
                                 log_path=tdir / f"transcript_r{rnd}.log")
                (tdir / f"transcript_r{rnd}.txt").write_text(
                    ar.to_json() + "\n---- TRANSCRIPT ----\n" + ar.transcript,
                    encoding="utf-8")
                changed, cand = commit_tree(tree, f"exp {tid} round {rnd}")
                ec.overlay_gold(tree, repo, t)   # ����ǰ�������ԣ�fix �棩
                gold = ec.eval_gold(tree, t)
                ec.remove_gold(tree, t)          # ������Ƴ�����й©�� agent��
                ev = None
                if arm != "A0" and changed:
                    ev = run_gate(tree, state_head, cand, tdir / f"gate_r{rnd}",
                                  {**t, "_arm": arm})
                tlog["rounds"].append({
                    "round": rnd, "adapter": {k: v for k, v in ar.__dict__.items()
                                              if k != "transcript"},
                    "committed": changed, "candidate": cand,
                    "gold": {k: v for k, v in gold.items() if k != "raw_tail"},
                    "gate_verdict": (ev or {}).get("verdict") if ev else None,
                })
                accepted = gold["ok"]
                if accepted or arm == "A0":
                    break
                if not changed and rnd == 1:
                    break  # agent û�����κθĶ����޸���������
            (tdir / "gold_result.json").write_text(
                json.dumps(gold, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
            if ev:
                (tdir / "gate_summary.txt").write_text(
                    evidence_summary(ev, arm), encoding="utf-8")
            code, diff = ec.gc.git(tree, "diff", f"{state_head}", "HEAD")
            (tdir / "diff.patch").write_text(diff, encoding="utf-8")
            tlog["accepted"] = accepted
            chain_log["tasks"].append(tlog)
            state_head = ec.gc.git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD")[1].strip()
            print(f"  [{tid}] {'ACCEPT' if accepted else 'REJECT'} "
                  f"(gold F{gold['counts']['failed']}/E{gold['counts']['errors']}"
                  f"/P{gold['counts']['passed']})", flush=True)
            (run_dir / "chain_log.json").write_text(
                json.dumps(chain_log, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False),
                encoding="utf-8")
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)  # �����ֿ⣬��Ŀ¼ֱ��ɾ
        meta["ended"] = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S")
        ec.write_meta(run_dir, **meta)

    n_ok = sum(1 for x in chain_log["tasks"] if x["accepted"])
    print(f"[done] {run_dir.name}: {n_ok}/{len(chain_log['tasks'])} accepted",
          flush=True)
    return run_dir


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--agent", required=True, choices=sorted(ADAPTERS))
    ap.add_argument("--model", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--arm", required=True, choices=ARMS)
    ap.add_argument("--chain", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--rep", type=int, default=1)
    ap.add_argument("--tasks", default=None, help="���ŷָ����� ID �Ӽ�")
    ap.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    only = [x.strip() for x in a.tasks.split(",")] if a.tasks else None
    run_chain(a.agent, a.model, a.arm, a.chain, a.rep, only, a.dry_run)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

+def test_issue801():
+    # strip_comments removes statements that consist only of comments
+    assert sqlparse.format("--A;--B;", strip_comments=True) == ""
+    assert sqlparse.format("--A;\n--B;", strip_comments=True) == ""
+    assert sqlparse.format("/* sql starts here */", strip_comments=True) == ""
+    assert sqlparse.format(
+        "/* sql starts here */\n/* or here */",
+        strip_comments=True, strip_whitespace=True) == ""
+    assert sqlparse.format(
+        "-- comment\nSELECT 1;", strip_comments=True) == "SELECT 1;"

def run(cmd: list[str], cwd: Path | None = None, env: dict | None = None,
        timeout: int = 1200) -> tuple[int, str]:
    import os
    full_env = {**os.environ, **env} if env else None
    try:
        p = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(cwd) if cwd else None, env=full_env,
                           capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8",
                           errors="replace", timeout=timeout)
        return p.returncode, (p.stdout or "") + "\n" + (p.stderr or "")
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return 124, "TIMEOUT"


def git(repo: Path, *args: str, timeout: int = 300) -> tuple[int, str]:
    return run(["git", *args], cwd=repo, timeout=timeout)


def changed_test_files(repo: Path, fix: str) -> list[str]:
    code, out = git(repo, "diff", "--name-only", f"{fix}^", fix, "--", *TEST_DIRS)
    if code != 0:

+def test_issue801():
+    # strip_comments removes statements that consist only of comments
+    assert sqlparse.format("--A;--B;", strip_comments=True) == ""
+    assert sqlparse.format("--A;\n--B;", strip_comments=True) == ""
+    assert sqlparse.format("/* sql starts here */", strip_comments=True) == ""
+    assert sqlparse.format(
+        "/* sql starts here */\n/* or here */",
+        strip_comments=True, strip_whitespace=True) == ""
+    assert sqlparse.format(
+        "-- comment\nSELECT 1;", strip_comments=True) == "SELECT 1;"
